Conversely, generative RMs leverage extensive reasoning to improve robustness on challenging tasks, but their natural language-based scores lack the numerical flexibility and probabilistic interpretability that scalar RMs offer. While recent approaches combine both paradigms through off-policy multi-task learning, such parallel optimization does not guarantee that generated reasoning traces actively align with or benefit downstream scalar reward prediction. To address this mismatch, we propose LatentRM, a reward modeling framework that learns intermediate reasoning traces as discrete latent variables to explicitly maximize the likelihood of downstream scalar rewards. Through on-policy optimization of the latent reasoning space end-to-end, LatentRM tightly couples deep reasoning-based evaluation with precise scoring. Extensive validations on in-distribution and OOD datasets and RLHF show that LatentRM outperforms scalar, generative, and hybrid RMs on preference modeling and policy alignment across tasks ranging from open-ended conversation to complex reasoning.

</details>


### [108] [Detecting Experiential Intertextuality Across Migration Routes: Beyond Surface Similarity in French Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29188)
*Sakayo Toadoum Sari,Nelly Robin,Michelle Auzanneau,Lakhdar Sais,Veronique Petit,Marie Veniard,Said Jabbour,Fabien Delorme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标注数据就能检测迁徙叙事中共同经历（experiential echoes）的方法，并比较了多种技术在法语迁徙叙事数据集上的表现，最终用监督混合模型取得了最佳相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动文本分析方法（如迁徙研究中的叙述分析），难以捕捉跨越不同迁徙路线中迁移者之间深层相似的经历（如暴力、剥削、危险等）。为推动对共同经历的理解与智能检测，作者提出了全新的“experiential intertextuality detection”任务。

Method: 作者基于108份法语迁徙叙事，从中自动生成句子对，并使用无监督方法打分，包括：词汇基线、句子嵌入、词性结构特征、迁徙主题词表、叙述上下文特征，以及对Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B两大LLM进行零样本/少样本提示。所有方法均对比了816个人类专家注释的共经历判断。

Result: 表面、结构、嵌入类方法与专家判断相关性均较低（r≤0.30）；Qwen2.5-7B零样本相关性最高（r=0.38）；少样本提示提升Mistral表现但反而降低Qwen表现；叙实体位置（如出发阶段）对共经历有重要预测价值；融合31个特征后的有监督混合模型效果最好（r=0.45），比单一最佳方法提升21%。

Conclusion: 传统无监督方法难以捕捉迁叙共经历，LLM零样本能力有限，模型融合和叙事位置特征可显著提升检测准确率。本文为迁徙叙事中的共经历文本分析提供了新方向和方法基础。

Abstract: Migrants traversing geographically distinct routes such as the Trans-Saharan and Balkan corridors often recount strikingly parallel lived experiences: police violence, smuggler exploitation, dangerous crossings, and family separation. We introduce the task of experiential intertextuality detection: automatically identifying shared experiential echoes across migration narratives without requiring annotated training data. From 108 French migration narratives spanning both corridors, we automatically generate sentence pairs and score them using annotation-free methods: lexical baselines, sentence embeddings, POS-based structural features, a migration-specific theme lexicon, context-aware narrative features, and zero-shot LLM scoring with Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B under three prompting strategies. We validate all methods against 816 expert-annotated intertextuality judgments (inter-annotator Krippendorff's $α= 0.27$). Our results reveal that all surface, structural, and embedding methods correlate only weakly with expert judgments ($r \leq 0.30$); Qwen2.5-7B zero-shot achieves the best single-method correlation ($r = 0.38$); few-shot examples degrade Qwen but dramatically improve Mistral; narrative position significantly predicts intertextuality, with departure-phase pairs showing the highest experiential echoes; and a supervised hybrid combining all 31 features achieves $r = 0.45$, a 21% improvement over the best individual method.

</details>


### [109] [Hy-MultiTurn: A Six-Dimensional Benchmark for Deep Multi-Turn Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29196)
*Eileen Ye,Jiawen Tao,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Wenhan Yu,Yaxin Fan,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Yanbing Jiang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hy-MultiTurn中文多轮对话理解基准，专门评估模型在真实场景下长多轮交互中的六大核心能力，并实验证明即使是最先进的模型表现依然有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮对话基准数据集大多覆盖较短对话，无法全面评估模型在处理长多轮交互（尤其是中文）时对细节记忆、条件控制、对象识别等复杂需求的能力。

Method: 作者分析了实际聊天机器人故障案例，总结出六种常见失败机制，进而设计了六种受控评测模式，针对约209个涉及12-76轮的长对话场景，涵盖约束记忆、精准执行、约束综合、对象定位、动作抑制、指代消解等能力。并对22种前沿大模型配置进行全面评测。

Result: 实验表明Hy-MultiTurn难度较高，即使是GPT-5.5等最先进模型，在所有对话要求下也仅有41.1%的任务完成率，且没有任一模型在六类任务表现均最佳。

Conclusion: 该基准更真实地反应了长多轮中文对话的挑战，暴露了现有模型对复杂对话需求的显著不足，为后续多轮对话建模与改进提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Long-running multi-turn interactions with chatbots and agents are now common, and a correct response often depends on remembering earlier details, tracking later revisions, identifying intended objects or referents, and withholding action when required conditions are unmet. Existing multi-turn benchmarks typically cover short exchanges and do not fully evaluate these capabilities in long multi-turn interactions, particularly in Chinese, while offering limited insight into how and why models fail. To address these limitations, we analyze real chatbot failures to identify six recurring mechanisms and use them to define six controlled evaluation modes in Hy-MultiTurn, a Chinese benchmark for deep multi-turn dialogue understanding. The six modes evaluate constraint memory, precise execution, constraint synthesis, object localization, action suppression, and reference resolution. Across the six modes, we construct 209 controlled tasks spanning 12-76 turns, with dialogue length, irrelevant-topic distraction, and colloquial phrasing adding further difficulty. Evaluation of 22 frontier model configurations shows that Hy-MultiTurn is broadly challenging, as even GPT-5.5, the strongest overall configuration, satisfies all requirements in only 41.1 percent of responses and no model performs best in all six modes.

</details>


### [110] [Knowing When to Quit: Diagnosing and Training LLMs to Abort Futile Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29211)
*Xinyan Guan,Jiali Zeng,Chunlei Xin,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型在超出能力范围的问题上经常产生看似合理实际无意义的推理，提出了CaRL方法，通过强化学习奖励模型拒绝无效推理，显著减少此类现象，提升模型的能力一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型面对超出其能力的问题时，常生成看似有道理但实际上错误的推理，这不仅对用户具有误导性，还可能造成实际风险，因此亟需方法让模型知道何时应拒答，以提升安全性与可靠性。

Method: 提出CaRL（能力对齐强化学习）方法，一方面通过奖励模型拒绝无效推理（reward shaping），另一方面通过失败案例增强拒绝监督（hindsight refusal augmentation），鼓励模型在能力范围外直接拒绝回答，避免产出虚假推理。

Result: 实验表明，经过CaRL训练的模型在各种任务难度下都显著减少了无效推理的发生率，同时保留了原有任务表现，实现了能力与行为高度一致。

Conclusion: CaRL方法能有效抑制大型模型能力外的无效推理，实现能力边界内的可靠输出，在不损失实用性的前提下提升了安全性与用户信任。

Abstract: Large language models generate computationally expensive yet semantically void reasoning on beyond-capability tasks, creating risks where plausible-sounding but incorrect derivations mislead users. We characterize this \textit{futile reasoning} phenomenon through systematic analysis, revealing universal capability overreach and systematic miscalibration between capability and behavior. The dominant failure mode is specious reasoning, which outputs look superficially valid but contain subtle errors, escalating with task difficulty. To address this, we introduce \textbf{CaRL} (\textbf{Ca}pability-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which aligns model behavior with capability boundaries through reward shaping that incentivizes refusal over futile reasoning and hindsight refusal augmentation that converts failures into refusal supervision. Experiments demonstrate a substantial reduction in futile reasoning while preserving performance across task difficulties, effectively achieving capability-aligned behavior without sacrificing utility. \footnote{https://github.com/icip-cas/Knowing-When-to-Quit}

</details>


### [111] [Small Is Enough: Per-User Style Rewriting of AI-Edited Text via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29238)
*Antorweep Chakravorty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InMyStyle 是一个强调隐私的单用户系统，通过微调小型语言模型，实现个性化重写 AI 编辑文本，无需推理时指令提示。其效果在不同模型尺寸下表现稳定，小模型也能胜任任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流文本重写个性化方法通常依赖云端大模型、需要用户提供指令，存在隐私和交互障碍。InMyStyle 旨在让本地小模型根据用户文档自动适应写作风格，实现隐私友好、无需人工指令的重写。

Method: 系统利用用户本地文档，借助多个本地助手型 LLM 构造训练样本，通过 LoRA 对 0.5B 至 7B 基座模型微调，采用长度感知生成和自动分段以支持不同输入长度。

Result: 基于 219 对科学论文数据，自动评分在不同模型规模/解码方式下均稳定在 0.69（满分 1），表明小模型就足够。辅助评价方面，LLM 评委也认为 AI 痕迹明显减少，且模型变大 AI 痕迹进一步降低。

Conclusion: 小型本地模型配合本地微调，能在保证隐私和无需额外指令下，有效实现用户风格的文本重写。系统表现对模型规模不敏感，小模型即可满足需求。

Abstract: InMyStyle is a privacy first, single user system that adapts small language models to rewrite AI-edited text towards an individual user's writing style without an instruction prompt at inference. Given a user's documents, it uses multiple local helper LLMs to construct paired training examples and fine tunes LoRA adapters on base models ranging from 0.5B to 7B parameters. Length aware generation budgets and automatic chunking support inputs of different lengths. On 219 evaluation pairs from a scientific-paper corpus, the automatic composite score plateaus at 0.69 [scale 0-1] across all model sizes under both greedy and sampled decoding. This observed plateau suggests that small models are sufficient for the measured rewriting task, with model size determining trade-offs rather than a stable quality ranking. As a secondary evaluation, 400 ratings from five LLM judges give InMyStyle outputs a mean perceived AI-ness score over 20% lower than their helper-AI generated inputs, while mean perceived AI-ness scores decrease with model size within InMyStyle.

</details>


### [112] [Data Turnstile: A Scalable Open Framework for Function-Calling Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29250)
*Goutham Ramakrishnan,Megha Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为Data Turnstile的开源框架，用于为小型语言模型（SLMs）生成高质量函数调用训练数据，显著提升其在工具使用相关任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型由于推理延迟低、成本和隐私优势，适合Agent场景部署，但在需要工具调用等任务上易受数据质量限制，尤其缺少高质量训练数据时效果较差。

Method: Data Turnstile能够接受用户定义的API规范，并自动生成高质量的函数调用训练数据。该框架将多轮工具交互分解为受约束的逐步生成过程，通过验证与错误反馈循环，严格控制API多样性、对话复杂性和输出正确性。

Result: 在两个函数调用基准测试中，使用Turnstile数据微调的小模型Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-1.7B表现显著提升，其中0.6B模型达到与思维链加持的大模型相近的性能，1.7B模型在多轮任务上超过比其大19倍的32B模型。

Conclusion: 通过高质量合成数据支持，小型语言模型在工具调用等任务上能大幅缩小与大模型的性能差距，Data Turnstile为SLM的实际应用提供了可行性和便利性。同时，论文公开了数据集和框架，有助于社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive for agentic deployment due to low latency, reduced cost, and on-device privacy, yet they struggle with tool-use tasks where training data is scarce and noisy. Unlike larger models, SLMs cannot compensate for low-quality supervision through sheer capacity, making data quality the critical bottleneck. We present Data Turnstile, an open-source framework that takes user-defined API specifications and generates high-quality synthetic training data for function calling. Turnstile decomposes multi-turn tool-use interactions into constrained, stepwise generation with validation and error-feedback loops, providing fine-grained control over API diversity, conversation complexity, and output correctness. We demonstrate effectiveness of domain adaptation with Turnstile data on two challenging function calling benchmarks. On the BFCL single-turn benchmark, a Qwen3-0.6B fine-tuned on Turnstile data without chain-of-thought achieves 75.9% overall accuracy (versus 67.4% for the base model with thinking enabled), closing the gap with thinking-enabled Qwen3-1.7B (78.4%) and Qwen3-4B (79.9%) despite being 3$\times$ and 7$\times$ smaller respectively. On $τ^2$-bench, a multi-turn agentic benchmark, Turnstile-trained Qwen3-1.7B achieves 31.1% pass^1 on the Telecom domain, improving 4.7$\times$ over its 6.6% base and surpassing Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct (27.4%), a model 19$\times$ larger. Turnstile-trained Qwen3-0.6B achieves 24.6%, improving 7$\times$ over its 3.5% base and approaching the 32B model (53$\times$ larger). We release Data Turnstile along with a dataset spanning 1,000+ APIs and 100K+ multi-turn interactions.

</details>


### [113] [CalibratedRubric: Task-Adaptive Rubric Banks for Open-Ended LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29252)
*Mengting Chen,Yanshu Sun,Wanting Liang,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Dezhi Chen,Jing Li,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的自适应评分框架 CalibratedRubric，有效提升了对开放式大模型输出的细粒度评估效率和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放式大模型（LLM）输出评估需要细致的评分标准，但人工设计代价高，且自动化方法存在辨识和可扩展性困境。传统方法依赖严格共识和二值过滤，难以精准区分有效和有信息量的评分维度。

Method: CalibratedRubric 结合了类型适应性评分、基于贝叶斯协议后验的可测性过滤，以及基于项目反应理论（IRT）的评分条目组装。该框架通过 Beta-Bernoulli 协议测量每个评分标准的可测性，并用子模信息覆盖目标筛选最具代表性的评分标准子集。

Result: 在人类评测一致性（JudgmentBench）上，将κ值从0.604提升到0.743。在多个任务和数据集上，仅需49个评分标准达到同等评估相关性（原需131个）。任务扰动实验显示自适应评分可有效减少系统误差。

Conclusion: CalibratedRubric 能高效、可靠且可扩展地对开放式 LLM 输出进行不确定性感知评价，有效提升评价质量，并依赖足够评审员数量确保结果稳健。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of open-ended LLM outputs requires fine-grained rubrics, yet expert curation is costly and difficult to scale. Existing automated pipelines rely on strict judge unanimity and binary variance filters, which cannot distinguish measurable rubrics from informative ones. We introduce CalibratedRubric, a task-adaptive framework that combines type-specific scoring, Bayesian rubric-measurability filtering, and item response theory (IRT)-based bank assembly. CalibratedRubric estimates each rubric's measurability with a Beta--Bernoulli agreement posterior and uses a submodular information-coverage objective to construct compact rubric banks over the observed capability range. Across financial, healthcare, general, and legal benchmarks, measurability filtering improves human-gold agreement on JudgmentBench from $κ=0.604$ to $0.743$. IRT-based greedy selection improves cross-fitted rank fidelity over random selection across all six evaluated response blocks and requires only 49 rather than 131 rubrics to reach the target correlation on FinResearchBench decision-support tasks. Task-label perturbations further reduce system separation, confirming the practical relevance of task-adaptive scoring. These results support CalibratedRubric as an efficient, uncertainty-aware approach to open-ended LLM evaluation, with calibration gains depending on sufficient judge redundancy.

</details>


### [114] [Translation with Thought: Difficulty-Adaptive Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Domain Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29287)
*Yongshi Ye,Biao Fu,Chongxuan Huang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TwT（Translation with Thought）框架，结合人类译者思维方式，自适应推理深度，实现多领域翻译的高效高质。对比其他主流模型，TwT在15个基准和多语言条件下翻译质量领先且推理成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域翻译需要不同程度的推理与复杂度，现有模型难以有效兼顾翻译质量与计算效率。受人类译者根据难度灵活调整思维深度的启发，尝试用更具认知效能的方法解决多领域翻译任务。

Method: （1）利用DeepSeek-R1提取的难度感知“思维链”，并由GPT-4o重写，使之更有人类思维经济，进行有监督微调；（2）结合翻译质量与推理效率的混合奖励进行强化学习优化。模型在Seen和Unseen多语言、多领域环境下，测试了不同规模和主干结构。

Result: 在15个基准、3个seen语言和59个unseen语言测试，以及三种主干模型消融实验下，TwT-7B和TwT-14B的翻译质量优于体量更大的SOTA推理翻译模型，且Token用量减少32-60%。

Conclusion: 将翻译推理过程与人类认知原则对齐，有助于提升多领域、多语言的泛化能力与翻译准确性，同时显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Multi-domain machine translation (MDMT) poses a unique challenge due to varying levels of linguistic complexity across domains. Inspired by human translators' ability to adapt reasoning effort based on difficulty, we propose TwT (Translation with Thought), a resource-rational framework that learns to modulate inference between intuitive and deliberate reasoning. TwT is trained in two stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on difficulty-aware long chain-of-thought traces distilled from DeepSeek-R1 and rewritten by GPT-4o to reflect human-like reasoning economy, and (2) reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward to optimize translation quality and reasoning efficiency. Evaluated on 15 benchmarks spanning in-domain and out-of-domain settings, as well as 3 seen and 59 unseen languages, with ablations across three backbone models, TwT-7B and TwT-14B outperform much larger SOTA reasoning models in translation quality, while reducing token usage by 32--60\%. These results confirm that aligning translation behavior with cognitive principles enables robust generalization, high translation quality, and efficient reasoning in MDMT.

</details>


### [115] [Cross-Lingual Transfer for Machine Translation in Turkic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29355)
*Omer Burak Cinar,Mehmet Mert Dalkilic,Cagri Toraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性分析了跨五种突厥语言（如土耳其语、阿塞拜疆语、乌兹别克语、哈萨克语和吉尔吉斯语）的迁移学习表现，总结了相关性最强的语言对，并对不同迁移方向、字母转写（Latinization）及其在模型和数据集间的稳定性进行了详细实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的机器翻译依赖于跨语言迁移，但在密切相关语系内部的迁移行为知之甚少。该研究旨在详细刻画和理解密切相关的突厥语族内部的迁移规律与效果。

Method: 作者采用mT5多语言模型，利用一套以转移源-目标对为基础的实验设计：每次微调模型时选择一种语言作为迁移源，评测另一种语言作为目标，翻译目标保持一致，结合pairwise转移矩阵观察具体转移效果。此外，实验比较了拉丁字母转写对于脚本不统一语言对的影响及不同数据集和模型设置下转移表现的稳定性。

Result: 突厥语言中，相关性强的语言对（如土耳其-阿塞拜疆，哈萨克-吉尔吉斯）之间的迁移效果最佳。转移方向（即哪种语言为源、哪种为目标）对结果有显著影响；同一语言对在不同翻译目标下表现不同。Latinization（字母转写）能提升部分脚本不统一情况的BLEU和chrF分数，但对所有评价指标的影响并不一致。不同数据集和模型条件下，迁移源的效果大多能保持稳定。

Conclusion: 密切相关语言间的迁移最有效，语言对的方向和翻译目标设计需重点考量。拉丁化处理对某些评测值有帮助，但效果需具体分析。突厥语内部迁移源的选择具有实用上的稳定性，为低资源但相关语种的机器翻译方法提供依据。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is central to low-resource machine translation, but its behavior within closely related language families remains insufficiently characterized. We study transfer among five Turkic languages; Turkish, Azerbaijani, Uzbek, Kazakh, and Kyrgyz; using pairwise transfer matrices. In this setting, each model is fine-tuned with one transfer source and evaluated on a different transfer target while the translation target remains the same. Across mT5 experiments, we find that transfer is strongest between closely related Turkic pairs, especially Turkish-Azerbaijani and Kazakh-Kyrgyz. We also show that transfer direction matters, and that the same transfer source-transfer target pair can behave differently when the translation target changes. Latinization improves BLEU and chrF in several script-mismatched settings, but its effect is not uniform across metrics. Additional analyses show that transfer sources are mostly stable across different datasets and model settings.

</details>


### [116] [Zero-Mem: Zero-Token Memory Operations for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29377)
*Yilin Xiao,Zhehan Zhu,Yujing Zhang,Jin Chen,Zijin Hong,Luyao Zhuang,Qinggang Zhang,Shengyuan Chen,Xiaocao Ouyang,Lingfei Ren,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Zero-Mem提出了一种无需消耗LLM token的内存操作方式，实现了在长对话中高效一致地检索记忆，仅在最终问答阶段调用LLM，显著提升了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有很多LLM Agent在需要长时序记忆时，往往需要多次调用LLM进行中间表述生成和检索操作，这带来了昂贵的token和时间成本，同时丢失或合并细节也会影响原始证据的溯源。

Method: Zero-Mem方法保留原始交互记录，通过构建实体-上下文图和时间层级结构，分别捕捉全局关联和会话局部性。对于每个查询，系统在这两种结构中检索并协调信息，只在最后的QA阶段调动LLM。通过确定性的数据筛选保持答案依赖真实轨迹。

Result: Zero-Mem在长记忆及长上下文的问答任务上，与其他方法表现持平甚至更优，但彻底去除了记忆相关的LLM调用和token消耗，且在相同QA设置下，内存操作时间减少了57.6%。消融实验验证了两种记忆结构及其动态协调的贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化的Agent记忆完全可以不依赖LLM生成中间表征。Zero-Mem显著提高了效率且保持了高性能，对构建高效长对话Agent具有重要意义。代码将在同行评审后公开。

Abstract: LLM agents need memory to act consistently over long interactions, yet many systems use additional LLM calls to operate that memory. Generating intermediate records and mediating their retrieval adds recurring token and time costs, while omitted or merged details can obscure the original evidence. We ask whether structured memory access requires generation at all. Zero-Mem introduces \emph{zero-token memory operations}: no step outside final question answering invokes an LLM or consumes LLM input or output tokens; encoder computation is accounted for separately. Zero-Mem preserves original interaction traces as its source of record. It organizes the traces in two complementary ways. An entity--context graph exposes connections across interactions, while a temporal hierarchy preserves conversational locality and session state. For each query, Zero-Mem weighs the two views, retrieves from both, and follows their structure to recover supporting relations or surrounding context. Deterministic calibration first discards conflicting evidence and then keeps the reader's answer grounded in the retrieved traces. Only the final-QA reader invokes an LLM. Across long-memory and long-context question-answering benchmarks, Zero-Mem achieves competitive performance while eliminating LLM calls and LLM-token consumption from memory operations. With the same final-QA reader and context budget, it reduces memory-operation time cost by 57.6\% relative to the fastest compared baseline. Ablations support the contribution of the two views and their query-dependent coordination. Overall, the results show that structured agent memory need not generate an intermediate representation of the past. After peer review, the code and implementation details will be available at \textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/TheMoon0815/Zero-mem}.

</details>


### [117] [PTP: Previous-Token Prediction based LLM Inversion for Near-Exact Prompt Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29378)
*Pirzada Suhail,Nagasai Saketh Naidu,Atanu R Sinha,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全新的语言模型逆向方法，可在黑盒设置下，仅利用目标LLM自身合成数据进行训练，实现较高精度的提示词重建，并优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM逆向方法依赖外部数据集、需访问模型权重或logits，且实现复杂，难以适用于只能黑盒调用的实际LLM服务。研究如何在仅能访问输出的条件下，有效重建输入提示词，对模型可解释性、攻击防御等有重要意义。

Method: 作者提出训练“逆语言模型（inverse language model）”，以回顾预测上一个token方式，在完全黑盒、无辅助信息条件下，用目标LLM自身生成的数据从零开始训练，建立前向和逆向生成过程的关联，可采样获得多样且语义等价的提示词。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多数据集、跨LLM迁移能力上均优于以往依赖大数据或权重的重建方法，并在提示词和响应重建的多项token级评估指标上取得更好表现。

Conclusion: 提出的方法无需模型内部信息，简化实现流程，拓宽了LLM逆向的应用场景，并在提示词重建任务上展示出兼顾准确性与通用性的突出优势，对后续黑盒LLM研究有重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text by auto-regressively sampling the next token. This inherently leads to a many-to-many mapping between prompts and responses, complicating the task of inferring prompts from observed outputs. Prior work on LLM inversion frames prompt recovery as a semantic reconstruction task. They rely on fine-tuning pretrained sequence-to-sequence models on large external datasets--and requiring access to model weights or logits--to generate semantically plausible prompts. In contrast, we present a functional approach to inverting a given LLM in a black-box setting, without auxiliary aids. We train an explicit inverse language model entirely from scratch on data synthetically generated from the target LLM itself. Analogous to forward next-token prediction, our inverse model is trained using previous-token prediction, establishing a generative link between the forward and inverse processes that enables faithful prompt reconstruction. Moreover, it naturally supports diverse prompt reconstructions through sampling, whereby all such prompts induce similar responses under the forward, target LLM. Our approach generalises across datasets and exhibits transferability in reconstructing prompts from responses generated by different LLMs. Further, across the set of token based evaluation metrics for prompt and response reconstructions, our approach outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [118] [Studying quantization trade-offs for efficient inference deployment in machine translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29397)
*Jim Zhao,Sohir Maskey,Koen Oostermeijer,Douglas Orr,Teryn Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在真实服务器环境中通过量化提升部署效率时，对延迟和吞吐量的实际影响，特别关注机器翻译模型在实际长文档、复杂负载下的量化取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署于服务器时，需兼顾高质量与低延迟。量化常被用于提升推理效率、降低显存，但相关工作少有在真实服务负载下对延迟、吞吐和翻译质量进行系统分析。

Method: 选取EuroLLM和Hy-MT2两类机器翻译模型（规模1.7B至22B），在A100/H100 GPU上，结合文档分块与W4A8/W8A8量化策略，系统评估不同负载下的延迟、吞吐与翻译质量（引入WMT24++文档级评测）；并与传统单句级评测进行对比分析。

Result: 文档分块和激进量化（W4A8/W8A8）可明显提升延迟-吞吐折中曲线。文档级评测显示，标准单句评测无法预测量化策略与长文档翻译的复杂关系。Hy-MT2模型对量化较为鲁棒，但EuroLLM在所有量化配置下质量大幅下降。

Conclusion: 推理效率与翻译质量的量化取舍，受量化格式和文本分块策略双重影响。实际部署需综合评估两者才能实现最优折中。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in realistic server environments poses challenges, as the system needs to provide high-quality responses with low latency. Quantization is a common approach to reduce the memory footprint and improve inference efficiency, yet its impact on latency and throughput is rarely evaluated under controlled, orchestration-level workloads. In this work we study the quantization trade-offs of two translation model families, EuroLLM \citep{martins2025eurollm} and Hy-MT2 \citep{zheng2026hy} across five models ranging from 1.7B to 22B for efficient deployment on a single A100 or H100 GPU. We demonstrate that combining a document-chunking strategy with W4A8 or W8A8 quantization improves the latency-throughput Pareto-curve under a wide range of workloads. Furthermore, since standard machine translation (MT) benchmarks rely on isolated sentences and fail to capture long-context dynamics, we introduce a document-level evaluation from WMT24++ to assess how text chunking strategies affect translation quality under quantization. Our results reveal that standard segment-level evaluation can fail to predict the interaction between quantization and long-context document translation. While Hy-MT2 remains robust under quantization, EuroLLM shows strong sensitivity and translation quality collapses rapidly for all considered quantization formats. Overall, our experiments show that the trade-off between inference efficiency and translation quality depends not only on the quantization format, but also on the choice of text chunking strategy.

</details>


### [119] [Know It, Act on It: Investigating Memory Utilization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29433)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Jianfei Ma,Emmanuele Chersoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型（LLM）在作为个性化伴侣时的记忆利用问题进行了系统性分析，提出了一种分离记忆回忆与行动利用的评估范式，并通过大规模实验揭示了模型能记住用户偏好却未能据此行动的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM作为个性化伴侣广受关注，但它们在准确利用用户偏好信息时常出现问题。具体而言，即使相关用户偏好信息在上下文中存在，LLM有时依然未能体现这些偏好。无法区分是模型'忘记'了偏好，还是'记住但未能利用'，制约了系统优化。

Method: 作者提出“Know and Act”分离评估范式：针对用户的同一个偏好，分别考察模型对偏好的回忆能力（Know测试）和在行为情境下是否能利用该偏好（Act测试）。在16个系统、5种记忆架构、1000条偏好、3种表达强度条件下做大规模实验。

Result: 实验显示LLM在记忆偏好（Know）与行为利用偏好（Act）之间存在显著差距——模型往往能记住用户偏好，却无法在实际情境中体现这一偏好。引入记忆架构后该差距有缩小，但在健康和心理治疗等高风险领域，偏好利用依然显著不足。

Conclusion: 记忆结构虽提升了LLM对用户偏好的利用能力，但模型在“知”与“用”之间仍有明显断层，尤其在敏感领域问题更突出，需进一步设计和优化以提升LLM个性化交互的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents evolve into personalized companions, memory has emerged as a core capability. However, LLMs face a knowledge utilization problem: they may fail to act on relevant user preferences even when they are fully present in context. When an agent fails to tailor its response in a context where previously shared user preferences should matter, it is unclear whether the model failed to remember that information or remembered it but failed to use it. To isolate this breakdown, we introduce a decoupled evaluation paradigm that administers paired Know and Act tests to the same user preference. We conduct large-scale experiments across 16 systems and five memory architectures, evaluating 1,000 preferences embedded at three levels of expression strength. Our results show a large gap between Know and Act outcomes: agents often pass the recall test for a user preference but fail to reflect that same preference in the paired behavioral scenario. While memory architectures reduce this gap, utilization remains especially weak for health and therapy-related preferences, where failures to act carry the greatest real-world stakes.

</details>


### [120] [Evidence-Type Competition: When Can Interventional Data Teach Language Models Causal Direction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29484)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，仅用干预数据进行训练并不能确保模型具备正确的因果推理能力，尤其在存在Simpson悖论的情景下，观测证据在推理阶段主导因果方向。模型能否使用干预证据取决于推理时上下文中的证据类型。消除观测证据能显著提升模型的因果推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推理能力对AI模型极其重要，而通常认为干预数据（即主动实验数据）是实现模型因果推理的金标准。论文希望测试这一假设，并探究在矛盾信息环境（如Simpson悖论）下，干预数据与观测数据谁在主导最终的模型推断。

Method: 作者在完全可控的合成环境中，设置了观测相关性和因果效应方向相反的Simpson悖论场景。通过不同干预数据与观测数据混合比例的训练设置，以及在推理阶段操控上下文证据类型，系统地考察了二者对模型因果推断方向的主导作用。还用激活打补丁的方式定位控制信号，并分析了采样误差和证据平均等评测方案。

Result: 结果显示，预训练时增加干预样本比例不能改善因果方向，模型因果答案的符号更多从观测证据中抄袭。推理时的上下文证据类型才决定因果方向。完全只给干预证据时因果方向正确率最高（41/50）。去除观测证据后，模型的因果能力明显提升，这种抑制效应在不同训练种子和不同参数规模下都很稳定。激活打补丁法定位到这种“开关”在中间层观测行上生效。

Conclusion: 作者指出，模型权重拥有进行因果推理的隐含能力，是否激活取决于推理时上下文。带观测证据的上下文会系统性压制模型的因果能力；采用证据平均等评测技巧可大幅减少符号错误。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [121] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个新基准ARB，用于检测AI文本检测器在LLM改写人类文本时的表现，发现主流检测器在传统基准上表现良好，但对LLM改写的人类文本检测性能大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往AI文本检测的基准只比较人类文本和直接LLM生成的文本，忽视了实际中LLM可能会改写人类内容。已有研究指出复写、改写会削弱检测效果，但仍缺乏针对“LLM改写人类文本”的系统性评估。因此本文提出新的基准，用于填补此类检测效果的评估空白。

Method: 作者构建了Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark（ARB），包含1800个人类原始文本，分别来自XSum、WritingPrompts和OpenWebText，每条文本用4种方式处理：人类原文、直接LLM生成、LLM改写人类文本、LLM改写LLM文本。采用4款LLM（Llama-3.2-3B、Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Gemma-2-9B）和5个检测器在1%误报率下，系统评测检测性能。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars-falcon-7b对直接LLM文本的检测率分别为91.2%和93.5%，但对LLM改写的人类文本检测率骤降至30.8%和15.1%。检测LLM改写LLM文本时仍有较高表现（78.3%/83.0%）。其他检测器如RADAR也表现类似，BERT-Defense和RoBERTa-Defense在所有场景下检测率都低于3%。

Conclusion: 传统的检测基准无法有效评估检测器对“LLM改写人类文本”的能力，检测器容易“漏检”此类文本，而对LLM自身改写的文本仍保持较高检测能力。结果表明，现有检测器在实际应用中面临严重局限，亟需专门面向此类“改写”场景的检测方法。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L). We evaluated five detectors (FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense) at a strict 1%-false-positive operating point (TPR@1%FPR). FastDetectGPT and Binoculars-falcon-7b detected 91.2% and 93.5\% of direct LLM text, but only 30.8% and 15.1% of human text an LLM had rewritten, a drop of 60-78 percentage points. The same detectors retained 78.3% and 83.0% recall when LLM text was rewritten by the same model, a much smaller decline of 10-13 points. RADAR followed the same pattern (66.8% to 12.2%), while BERT-Defense and RoBERTa-Defense stayed below 3% recall across all regimes. These results show that detector performance measured on the conventional human-vs-LLM benchmark does not transfer to human-authored text revised by an LLM, even though the same detectors remain largely robust to LLM-only rewriting.

</details>


### [122] [Sycophancy Undermines Epistemic Vigilance in Cooperative Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29585)
*Rupak Sarkar,Neha Srikanth,Saloni Gupta,Claire Bonial,Philip Resnik,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文探讨了大模型在合作对话中的"拍马屁"倾向，即信息不对称对话中模型更倾向于同意对方而忽视自身证据，提出了一项基于协作"找不同"对话任务来评估视觉-语言模型的认知警觉性，通过特定方法对模型进行引导显著减少了相关问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂合作任务中，AI需要像人类一样，有能力评估对方信息与自身证据的冲突，否则容易成为不可靠的合作伙伴。现实中大模型在合作对话场景下往往表现出过度顺从，缺乏必要的证据审查能力，亟需有效的衡量与改进方法。

Method: 设计了一项信息不对称的对话型找不同任务：两个视觉-语言模型分别看不同图片，通过对话判断图片是否相同，或指出不同之处。实验中分析模型是否会为无原则同意对方而忽略自身观察内容。并进一步应用针对"拍马屁"倾向的矢量对模型进行能力引导以减少此类错误。

Result: 实验结果显示：主流视觉-语言模型常忽略自身信息，倾向于同意对方，显示出明显的"拍马屁"（sycophancy）行为。使用从任务无关的例子学习到的矢量进行引导后，模型的证据忠实度显著提升，相关错误有效减少。

Conclusion: 通过系统设计测评与模型能力引导，能够显著提升模型在合作对话中的理据坚持与信息忠实度，为提升AI在信息不对称协作任务中的可靠性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: To maintain common ground in cooperative conversation, humans iteratively update their beliefs as conversation participants share new information; participants who are epistemically vigilant detect when new information conflicts with prior beliefs and take steps to repair these conflicts. In order for AI systems to serve as reliable partners in complex cooperative tasks, they must similarly weigh incoming information against their own private evidence and shared context and appropriately surface inconsistencies when they arise. To measure the epistemic vigilance of vision-language models in cooperative settings, we present an information-asymmetric, dialog-based "spot-the-difference" task. Two models are privately shown one image each, and must determine through conversation whether the images are identical or, if not, identify the difference. Models routinely fail at this: they frequently overlook key evidence in their private image in favor of agreeing with their conversational partner, even when their agreement is unwarranted. We relate these violations of epistemic vigilance to the broader behavior of sycophancy, which manifests itself in cooperative goal-oriented dialog as over-accommodation and weak evidential grounding. Our results show that model steering to reduce sycophancy with a vector learned from task-agnostic sycophancy examples can reduce epistemic vigilance-related errors, making models more faithful reporters of their evidence, and in turn, more reliable partners in information-asymmetric cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [123] [ResKV: Reconstructing Omitted Attention Contributions for Fixed-Budget KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29591)
*Yuhang Zhan,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的KV缓存压缩方法ResKV，在保持推理效率的同时更好地保留了被丢弃token对注意力的贡献。实验结果显示该方法能在多种测试集和参数设置下提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存驱逐方法会永久舍弃未被选择的token，丧失其对注意力的整体贡献。虽然有合并方案试图保留更多信息，但会影响应保持精准的key/value。这导致长上下文推理时，推理效果下降，本工作旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 作者观察到，驱逐掉的信息可以形式化为softmax注意力分子的残差统计量。基于此，ResKV方法将固定KV缓存预算分为主缓存（精确）和残差缓存（紧凑、重建被舍弃token的贡献），主缓存和残差条目共同参与softmax归一化过程，并设计了训练时残差分配代理和推理时的动态门控来精细管理残差贡献。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER等真实场景下（含多种主干模型、缓存预算与压缩基线）、包括面向查询和非查询等多种设置下，ResKV均有较大幅度的全局改进，并保持峰值内存和长文本推理效率。

Conclusion: ResKV在不增加内存/延迟的前提下，提升了KV缓存压缩场景下的推理性能，为高效处理长上下文任务提供了新方法。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for efficient long-context inference. Existing eviction methods permanently discard unselected tokens and consequently remove their aggregate contribution to attention. Merging-based alternatives preserve more information but can perturb retained keys and values that should remain exact. We observe that the information omitted by cache eviction can be formulated as residual statistics in both the numerator and denominator of softmax attention. Based on this observation, we propose ResKV, which divides a fixed KV budget into an exact main cache and a compact residual cache that reconstructs the contribution of omitted tokens. ResKV lets main-cache tokens and residual entries participate in the same softmax normalization, so residual entries restore both attention numerator and denominator mass rather than acting as a post-hoc correction. A construction-time validation proxy determines residual allocation for each layer and KV head, while a decode-time dynamic gate adjusts residual contributions for individual queries. Comprehensive evaluations on LongBench and RULER, covering query-aware and query-agnostic settings, multiple backbones, cache budgets, and representative compression baselines, demonstrate broad improvements under the same retained KV budget while preserving the practical efficiency of compressed decoding, including peak memory usage and long-context decode throughput.

</details>


### [124] [FriendBench: Benchmarking Dyadic Familiarity Inference in Humans and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29602)
*Jeffrey M. Girard,Jason Z. Zheng,Jacqueline R. Vertino,Antony D'Avirro,Benjamin Peloquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FriendBench基准数据集，通过20秒双人破冰对话视频，判断两人是熟人还是陌生人，并评估了26种模型与人类在该任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解社交场景常常依赖于行为而非语言，现有方法缺乏对此能力的系统性评估，作者希望通过构建基准数据集推动相关研究。

Method: 收集既有熟人也有陌生人参与固定问答的20秒对话数据，涵盖文本、音频、视频三种模态，并让人类小组与26个业内模型分别对96组均衡样本进行判断，比较精度及决策偏好。

Result: 最优模型与人类在所有模态下精度无显著差异，但模型更偏向于判断为“陌生人”，而人类判断均衡。此外，增加模态信息有助于提升表现，但仅人类能有效利用视频行为信息。

Conclusion: 模型已能达到人类精度水平，但判断动机不同，人类在社会行为理解中仍有独特优势。作者公开相关数据和预测结果，推动后续研究。

Abstract: Reading a social situation often depends on behavior, not words alone. We introduce FriendBench, a benchmark for inferring whether two people are already familiar or are meeting as strangers, from a 20-second clip of a dyadic ice-breaker conversation. Every pair answers the same type of prompt, so only the manner of interaction can reveal the answer. Across text, audio, and video, we compare 26 models from seven companies against matched human panels over 96 balanced dyads. The best model and the human crowd are statistically indistinguishable on accuracy in every modality, but reach it differently: humans stay balanced across the two answers, while the strongest models lean toward "stranger"---a difference in effective prior, not discrimination. Richer channels help both unequally, and only humans gain from visible behavior on top of speech. We release the stimuli, human ratings, and model predictions.

</details>


### [125] [Evolving language compositionality in a frequency-structured meaning space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29642)
*Fabio De Ponte,Eloise Gaines-White,Conor Houghton,Seth Bullock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 作者探讨了意义出现频率对语言迭代学习模型下组成性（compositionality）演化的影响，发现频率分布单元不同会导致语言结构稳定性的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究已显示通过代际传递会自发生成组成性语言，但现实自然语言中一些高频意义的表达方式不遵循一般语法规则，因此研究意义频率如何影响语言组成性的演化。

Method: 作者操控语言中不同意义的出现频率，使部分意义远高于其他意义，同时分别在‘整体意义向量’和‘部分单元’两个层级施加频率结构，然后观察语言在多代学者间的传递及其组成性结构变化。

Result: 结果显示，高频整体意义能在一定程度上逃避一般语法压力，保留特殊表达方式；但如果频率结构加在部分单元而非整体意义上，即便高频单元易习得，语言整体却难以代际传递。

Conclusion: 只有当频率分布在学习者可整体习得的“形式-意义单元”时，频率才能塑造可持续的语言结构；而对小单元进行频率调控时，无法支持语言的组成性及稳定传递。

Abstract: The iterated learning model was introduced to investigate language evolution: the way in which the characteristic properties of human languages have been shaped, at least partly, by repeated transmission from one language user to another. The key finding is that language compositionality can arise spontaneously as a consequence of language being passed repeatedly through a language learning bottleneck. Here we explore how changing the frequency of different meanings, so that some meanings occur much more frequently than others, affects the character of its compositionality. We find that, as observed in natural languages, high-frequency meanings can escape the pressure to conform to the grammar that characterizes lower-frequency meanings. However, when the frequency structure is instead imposed on parts rather than on whole meaning vectors, the language fails to transmit across generations. This occurs despite the fact that the most frequent elements are reliably learned. These results suggest that frequency can shape emergent linguistic structure only when the frequency distribution is defined over form-meaning units that learners can acquire holistically. When frequency is instead distributed over smaller units, it fails to support the relational structure required for compositional generalisation, thereby preventing stable language transmission.

</details>


### [126] [TokTier: Exact Stateful Tokenization for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29678)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了TokTier，一种高效的有状态分词服务，显著加速了大语言模型前端处理流程，尤其是在多轮长文本交互场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型服务系统虽然对KV状态做了缓存，但前端每次调用仍需对完整请求文本重新分词，造成大量重复计算，尤其在代码智能体等需要频繁提交长上下文的小变动时极其低效。

Method: TokTier作为分词服务，保证每次输出的token与全量分词一致。对于会话续接，仅重新分词末尾变动窗口，通过稳定边界校验拼接，不可用时才全量分词。对无可复用前缀的情况，TokTier将GPT系列正则预分词分解为局部规则并用GPU加速执行；采用抽样验证防止错误。

Result: 覆盖17种分词器家族，实测分词增量修复耗时仅为0.5-1.1毫秒（100K-3M字符），比现有最优方法最高快437倍，完整GPU分词1M字符仅0.87毫秒。部署vLLM系统下，首token响应时间P50提升16-34%，P99提升23%。四核修复加一GPU每秒可处理1821请求，性能远超16核无状态前端。

Conclusion: TokTier极大加速了LLM前端分词流程，在实际长对话及智能体场景下效率与吞吐率大幅提升，可作为大模型推理系统高性能基础设施。

Abstract: LLM serving systems cache prompt KV state, yet most front ends still re-tokenize the full request text on every call. The cost lands on coding agents, which resubmit a long transcript after each small tool result, and reuse is hard because even a short append can change token boundaries near the end of the previous sequence. Across 153,951 calls from two agent ecosystems, the median call appends about 1.4K characters, and only 1.0-3.6% of calls start or rebuild a session with contexts of millions of characters. At a 94.1% fleet prompt-cache hit rate, tokenization reaches up to 64% of time to first token.
  TokTier is a stateful tokenization service with one contract: emitted token IDs are always identical to full reference tokenization of the request text. For a session continuation, it re-tokenizes a small window around the append and splices only after a per-request stable-boundary check, widening the window or falling back to full tokenization on failure. For a call without a reusable prefix, it decomposes GPT-family regex pre-tokenization into run-local rules and runs exact pre-tokenization and BPE on a GPU. A sampled shadow verifier re-checks live traffic.
  Across 17 tokenizer families, differential campaigns cover 1.5x10^10 split checks, a 12.4 TB real-text corpus, and 93,000+ replayed agent steps, with zero divergence. Incremental repair takes 0.5-1.1 ms from 100K to 3M characters, up to 437x faster than HF tokenization and 2.1x faster at 1M than the strongest cache-based baseline (Gigatoken) fully prewarmed. GPU full tokenization encodes a 1M-character request in 0.87 ms, up to 491x below HF and 23.4x below the fastest published CPU method. With vLLM, median time to first token drops 16-34% and P99 drops 23% under recorded bursts. Under a 50 ms P99 objective, four repair cores plus one GPU sustain 1,821 requests/s where a 16-core stateless front end saturates at 40.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Advances, challenges, and opportunities for legged robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28952)
*Jonas Frey,Matías Mattamala,Hae-Won Park,Mayank Mittal,Georg Martius,Maike Osborne,Robert Sparrow,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了两足与四足机器人在硬件、运动、自主性、数据与应用等方面的现状、挑战与未来展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能机器人日益融入社会，了解其对社会的影响及其促进科学发现的潜力变得重要。作者旨在分析两足与四足智能机器人的能力与面临的问题。

Method: 通过对当前机器人硬件、运动能力、自主性、数据处理能力及典型应用的梳理，结合最新研究进展，归纳出主要技术挑战，并探讨未来的发展方向。

Result: 文章总结了腿式机器人的多项最新进展，同时明确指出关键难题仍待解决，如硬件可靠性、自主决策、数据利用等。

Conclusion: 推进腿式机器人广泛应用需解决现存挑战，并需兼顾伦理、经济、政策及社会影响等因素。

Abstract: Humanoid and quadrupedal robots have the potential to revolutionize the way we work, interact, and coexist with intelligent machines. To understand their effects on society and how they can enable scientific discovery, we assess the current capabilities of these systems along hardware, locomotion, autonomy, data, and applications. We identify recent advances and key open challenges that must be overcome to enable widespread adoption and new use cases for legged robots. Last, we provide an outlook on the future of legged robots, exploring their ethical considerations, economic potential, policy implications, and broader societal effects.

</details>


### [128] [ST-WAM: Semantic-Temporal World Action Model for Robust Manipulation under Visual Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28993)
*Mingxin Wang,Bin Hu,Bin Qian,Kaitao Jiang,Haoning Wu,Feng Yan,Bowen Jing,Ruiyang Hao,Enyi Wang,Kangning Niu,Yandan Yang,Mu Xu,Yan Wang,Houde Liu,Tianlun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的世界动作模型（ST-WAM），通过引入DINOv3特征提升了机器人在视觉干扰下的任务鲁棒性，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型依赖于像素级未来生成，易受视觉分布变化影响导致泛化能力不足。作者发现，模型在遇到视觉变化时往往会生成与当前场景无关的内容，从而影响下游任务表现。

Method: 作者提出了语义-时序世界动作模型（ST-WAM），利用DINOv3语义特征进行未来预测和历史检索，并保持基于VAE的细粒度动力学。具体包括DSFE模块（同时预测VAE潜变量与DINO特征）和CAIR模块（在当前视觉-语言上下文下，从DINO历史中检索任务相关信息）。模型实现端到端训练，无需额外本体预训练或任务特定标注，推理阶段也无需显式生成未来图像。

Result: ST-WAM在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0两大基准测试中分别取得98.7%和92.8%的成绩。与Fast-WAM方法相比，零样本场景下LIBERO-Plus性能提升21.3个百分点，在真实世界视觉变化下成功率从25.8%提升到61.5%。

Conclusion: 语义-时序建模有效增强了像素生成式动力学模型的鲁棒性，提高了机器人在视觉变化场景下的任务表现。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm by jointly modeling robot actions and future visual dynamics. However, their reliance on pixel-generative future supervision can entangle action-relevant state transitions with task-irrelevant visual content, limiting robustness under visual distribution shifts. We identify Training-Distribution Hallucination, a recurring phenomenon in which futures conditioned on visually shifted observations hallucinate training-domain content rather than remain faithful to the current scene. A controlled frame-triplet diagnosis further shows that DINOv3 features remain more stable across visual shifts while better preserving task-state distinctions than Wan-VAE latents. Rather than correcting the predicted futures, we propose Semantic-Temporal WAM (ST-WAM) to improve action robustness by using DINOv3 as a shared semantic representation for future prediction and history retrieval while retaining fine-grained VAE dynamics. Its Dual-Space Future Experts (DSFE) jointly predict future VAE latents and DINO features, while Current-Anchored Intent Retrieval (CAIR) retrieves task-relevant evidence from recent DINO history under the current visual-language context. ST-WAM is trained end-to-end without additional embodied pretraining or task-specific annotations, and requires no explicit future generation at inference. It achieves 98.7% on LIBERO and 92.8% on RoboTwin 2.0; more importantly, compared with Fast-WAM, it improves zero-shot LIBERO-Plus performance by 21.3 percentage points and more than doubles real-world success under visual shifts from 25.8% to 61.5%. These results demonstrate that semantic-temporal modeling effectively complements pixel-generative dynamics for robust manipulation.

</details>


### [129] [Receding-Horizon Next-Best-View Planner for Autonomous Leaf Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28995)
*Arif Ahmed,Sajal K. Das,Parikshit Maini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向植物叶片表面重建的下一最佳视点（NBV）规划方法，通过新颖的信息增益函数，有效提高了重建的精度，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模田间植物监测和性状分析依赖高精度叶片重建，自动化机器人受到路径和计算资源限制，因此急需一个在有限规划预算下最大化重建质量的NBV选择策略。

Method: 提出了一种基于质心的信息增益函数（CIG），通过度量已观测点相对于点云质心的空间分布来评价视点价值，并设计了前视（receding-horizon）变体，能更好地考虑未来视点的重建贡献。方法在公开的草莓数据集上与基于注意力的NBV方法进行对比实验。

Result: 所提方法在多个植物生长阶段和高遮挡情况下，重建误差明显低于基线，表面几何保真度更高。前视NBV策略的重建误差最高可降低10%。

Conclusion: 本文创新性的方法能在机器人路径和资源受限时，有效优化叶片表面点云重建，对精确农业和自动化监测具有较强实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate plant leaf modeling is fundamental to downstream tasks such as plant growth monitoring, and phenotyping for yield estimation. Autonomous robotic reconstruction for large-scale field deployment must address limitations on robot planning budget and computation resources while optimizing viewpoint utility for leaf surface reconstruction. Existing approaches either focus on rigid objects, point-cloud coverage or plant reconstruction without fully addressing the system limitations or exploiting task-driven point cloud utility. In this work, we study next-best-view (NBV) planning for leaf surface reconstruction under travel constraints. We develop a novel Centroid-based Information Gain (CIG) function that measures the spatial distribution of observed points relative to the centroid of the existing point cloud to compute viewpoint utility. We also develop a receding-horizon variant that reasons over future viewpoints. To benchmark our work, we use the LAST-STRAW [1] public dataset that includes point clouds of strawberry plants over different growth stages and compare our method with attention-driven NBV [2] that uses a visibility-based information gain approach. The proposed receding-horizon approach consistently reduces surface reconstruction error and improves geometric fidelity across multiple growth stages, especially under increased inter-leaf occlusion. Results demonstrate that our approach is able to visit viewpoints that reduce surface reconstruction error and improves reconstruc-tion accuracy as compared to the baseline by upto 10%.

</details>


### [130] [D-VLC: Decentralized Vision-Language Collaboration for Heterogeneous Embodied Multi-Robot Systems in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29009)
*Yuan Zhou,Ruitong Lin,Shen Wang,Weiqi Gai,Mo zhu,Xin Zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合去中心化异步推理、轻量级信息共享、能力感知协作和统一动作接口的多机器人系统框架，能以通用视觉语言模型（VLM）指导异构机器人完成复杂任务，无需针对特定任务或机器人训练。实验表明新方法泛化性强，任务成功率高，效率优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的多机器人系统难以理解复杂语义指令，且对机器人和任务高度特化，缺乏泛化能力。现有基于大语言/视觉语言模型（LLM/VLM）的方法又大多依赖已知地图和集中决策，难以应对异构机器人和未知任务。因此，论文旨在解决多机器人系统的泛化与适应性问题。

Method: 本文提出去中心化、异步的推理框架，各机器人通过轻量级数据共享，基于VLM理解任务并根据各自能力协作，通过统一接口将自然语言或视觉结果转化为动作指令，实现无须专门训练的泛化式协作。

Result: 在不同场景和使用多种VLM的实验中，所提方法任务完成率超过70%，任务完成时间较几何贪婪基线减少最多55.8%。

Conclusion: 论文验证了以通用VLM驱动的去中心化、异步多机器人系统在复杂、未知环境任务中的泛化能力和高效率，展示了无需特殊训练即可进行有效协作的潜力。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems, particularly heterogeneous robot swarms, can improve the efficiency of complex task execution through parallel collaboration and complementary capabilities. However, conventional rule-based methods rely on predefined task models and specialized decision making programs, making it difficult to understand complex semantic instructions and coordinate heterogeneous robots. LLMs introduce strong language understanding and task reasoning capabilities, allowing multi-robot systems to interpret instructions, decompose tasks, and assign roles according to task semantics. VLMs further incorporate visual perception, enabling robots to reason about objects, regions, and spatial relationships in physical environments. Nevertheless, existing LLM/VLM based methods often depend on known maps, centralized and synchronized decision making, limiting their generalization to heterogeneous robots and unseen tasks. We therefore propose a framework that combines decentralized asynchronous reasoning, lightweight information sharing, capability aware collaboration, and a unified action interface, enabling general purpose VLMs to generate robot specific actions executed by learning free experts without task or robot specific training. Experiments across diverse scenarios and multiple VLMs show success rates above 70\%, with completion time reduced by up to 55.8\% relative to the geometric greedy baseline.

</details>


### [131] [DART: Dual-Axis Airborne Reachability-Gated Torque-Reaction for Off-Road Vehicle Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29011)
*Yu Hu,Fangzhou Zhao,Mingyuan Sang,Cheng Min,Liang Chen,Wei Li,Wenyu Kuang,Shican Chen,Jinwei Li,Baolei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了高速越过起伏地形时，车辆腾空后的控制难题，提出DART控制方法，并在仿真中大幅提升着陆安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速行驶的车辆在越过丘陵、台阶或坑洼地形时，常常被弹至空中。腾空后落地若控制不当，极易发生严重事故。因此，研究如何安全回收和控制腾空车辆的姿态，具有重要实际意义。

Method: 通过理论分析和仿真，作者定量揭示了在飞行阶段车辆基于车轮的角动量调节能力极为有限，仅能在有限范围内修正俯仰速率。提出DART（双轴空中可达性门控力矩反应）方法，将落地约束反向推导得到闭式可行起跳集，形成起跳前速度调节律和飞行中俯仰/横滚控制律，并通过仿真进行验证。

Result: 仿真结果显示，DART法能显著减少着陆速度36%，使满分着陆率从0/30提升至30/30。复杂路况下，DART带来29/30次安全着陆，远超现有RW-PD与TOBB方法。在倾斜路面上，DART将俯仰误差控制在2°以内。

Conclusion: 腾空车辆在飞行相位的姿态调整能力有限，关键控制节点在于起跳前。DART系统能显著提升着陆安全和姿态控制效果，验证了理论设定的有效性。当前结果基于仿真，实际硬件验证尚待开展。

Abstract: Traversing crests, ledges, and ditches at high speed often launches vehicles into the air, and a mishandled landing presents a substantial crash hazard. We show that the airborne phase is barely controllable: on a 1383 kg platform the wheel angular-momentum budget caps the recoverable pitch-rate change at roughly $9$-$13^\circ$/s in the tighter nose-up direction under drive at typical takeoff wheel speeds, and at about twice that in the reverse-inclusive braking direction; driving the wheels to their drivetrain hard limit raises the measured nose-up ceiling to only $16$-$18^\circ$/s. Takeoff pitch-rate disturbances beyond this directional budget are physically unrecoverable in flight, so the decisive leverage lies before takeoff. DART (Dual-Axis Airborne Reachability-Gated Torque-Reaction) back-propagates the landing constraint into a closed-form certified feasible-takeoff set, which supplies a conservative go/no-go condition and a pre-takeoff speed-shaping law. In flight, DART regulates pitch and roll via steer-resolved wheel-reaction torque, governed by a per-flight roll latch derived from the yaw-coupling analysis. In deterministic full-scale simulation in BeamNG.tech, a calibrated pre-takeoff speed regulator reduces touchdown speed by 36% and raises on-target landings from 0/30 to 30/30. Under the same steep-lip approach the airborne law completes 29/30 safe landings under crash-avoidance bounds versus 0/30 for reaction-wheel-style PD (RW-PD) and time-optimal bang-bang (TOBB). On banked run-ups DART holds the median pitch error at or below $2^\circ$ at every cross-slope, with the largest baseline separation at $γ=12^\circ$. Across disturbance regimes, the latch preserves pitch-only allocation on low-disturbance entries and enables dual-axis control when roll becomes binding. All results are from simulation; hardware validation remains open.

</details>


### [132] [Auto-JEPA: A Latent World Model of Continuous Intent for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29031)
*Jiwei Yang,Zhengxian Chen,Chaosheng Huang,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向行动的自动驾驶世界模型Auto-JEPA，主张预测未来驾驶意图而非重建整个未来世界，并且在多个基准任务上取得了优异成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶的世界模型往往过度依赖对未来环境的全面重建，如未来视频、占用状态等。本文认为用于规划只需关注将影响未来自车动作的场景特征，无需密集地预测整个未来世界。

Method: 提出Auto-JEPA模型，输入视觉观测、历史自车运动和导航指令，输出对未来驾驶意图的嵌入表示。该意图嵌入与未来轨迹的潜在表示对齐，然后从固定轨迹库中检索可执行轨迹，由场景条件匹配模块进行排序。视觉编码器保持冻结状态，无需显式感知标签和学习式轨迹生成器。仅优化与任务关联的模块。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1获得91.3 PDMS分数，在NAVSIM v2获89.1 EPDMS分数。遮挡动态要素实验表明，遮挡会显著影响意图变化，而遮挡与未来驾驶无关的车辆基本不影响结果。

Conclusion: Auto-JEPA聚焦于与规划相关的视觉特征，实现了高质量规划而无需密集预测未来世界，验证了以行动为导向的未来意图预测在自动驾驶规划中的有效性。

Abstract: Existing autonomous-driving world models typically perform dense prediction of future videos, occupancy states, BEV representations, or agent motion. We argue that planning need not reconstruct the complete future world, but only focus on scene features that affect future ego action. Based on this perspective, we propose Auto-JEPA, an action-oriented latent world model that learns continuous future driving intent through joint-embedding prediction. Given visual observations, egomotion history, and navigation commands, Auto-JEPA predicts an intent embedding aligned with the latent representation of the future ego trajectory. The predicted intent retrieves executable trajectories from a fixed trajectory memory, which are then ranked by a scene-conditioned candidate selection module. Auto-JEPA keeps the visual encoder frozen, requires no explicit perception annotations, and uses no learned trajectory generator. By optimizing only task-specific modules for trajectory representation, intent prediction, and candidate selection, Auto-JEPA achieves 91.3 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2. Semantic occlusion experiments show that masking dynamic-agent regions induces an average intent change 2.97x that of equal-area random masking. Moreover, occluding vehicles that affect future driving substantially changes the predicted intent and selected trajectory, whereas both remain essentially unchanged when non-influential vehicles are occluded. These results show that future-intent prediction encourages the model to focus on planning-relevant visual features and supports high-quality planning without dense future-world modeling.

</details>


### [133] [Outcome-Guided Distillation: A Teacher-Student Framework to Advance VLM Reasoning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29052)
*Zeyu Dong,Yimin Zhu,Yu Wu,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将结构化推理和几何精度结合的端到端自动驾驶方法，通过教师-学生架构提升可解释性和驾驶鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶虽然能直接从视觉到控制，但模型不可解释且在复杂场景下表现不佳。为提升可解释性和鲁棒性，当前方法引入视觉-语言模型，却依赖高成本、易出错的人工标注。本研究旨在无需中间标注的前提下，提升模型泛化能力和性能。

Method: 提出一种新框架，采用教师-学生架构。教师模型利用VLM进行反思推理，通过生成和修正推理文本以辅助行动决策，并用真实动作监督反思；学生通过监督微调学习教师的推理能力；设计了独立的waypoint译码器，将推理文本转换为轨迹。融合了解释性推理与高性能驾驶目标。

Result: 在Waymo基准数据集上，该方法在零样本推理、waypoint精度及推理效率上均优于传统基线。推理文本对驾驶推理有显著帮助，带来约24%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 本方法推动了自动驾驶系统向可解释和可部署方向发展，验证了推理驱动设计在自动驾驶中的有效性，并实现了性能和解释性的双重提升。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving aims to learn a direct mapping from visual observations to control actions. However, these E2E models often act as black boxes and struggle with complex scenarios. To address this, recent works incorporate Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to provide explicit reasoning, enhancing both interpretability and driving robustness. These approaches typically rely on pre-generated annotations, which suffer from potentially flawed labels and require costly human labor. In this work, we propose a new framework that integrates structured reasoning and geometric precision through a teacher-student architecture. The teacher model introduces reflective reasoning, where the VLM generates logical explanations and then reflectively refines the reasoning under the supervision of ground-truth action. This enhances zero-shot generalization without intermediate labels. The student model distills the teacher's reasoning capabilities via supervised fine-tuning. We also design a separate waypoint decoder that interprets textual reasoning into continuous trajectories. Our proposed solution integrates two goals: providing explicit reasoning for interpretability and delivering robust and accurate driving performance. It leverages the synergy between these two objectives within a staged inference engine to enhance driving performance and explicitly uses the reasoning to guide driving prediction. Evaluated on Waymo benchmarks, our framework outperforms classical reasoning-based baselines in zero-shot reasoning, waypoint accuracy, and inference efficiency. Our experiments validate this design, demonstrating that the reasoning text makes a significant contribution to driving inference, resulting in around a 24% improvement in performance compared to an identical model that lacks reasoning. Our work advances reasoning-driven autonomous driving toward interpretable and deployable systems.

</details>


### [134] [VSTaI: Design and Characterization of Variable-Stiffness Tactile Interfaces Based on 3D-Printed Structured Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29102)
*Yiting Mo,Xinyuan Mao,Jashan Preet Singh,Fernando Bello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于结构织物真空夹持的可变刚度触觉界面（VSTaI），实现了可调触觉刚度显示，并有望应用于仿真体检训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的体格检查（触诊）训练需要能够实时、真实反映软组织刚度变化的模拟装置，而现有模拟器难以做到既可靠又高效地调节触觉刚度。作者希望开发一种新型设备来满足这一需求。

Method: 设计了一种由两层硅胶夹裹一层真空密封3D打印结构织物的复合界面，通过调控内部压力，实现刚度调节。设计并测试了四种不同几何参数的织物结构，在大气和真空状态下进行力-压缩实验评估其性能。

Result: 实验表明，真空夹持可以显著提升触觉刚度，有效模量可从亚兆帕提升到兆帕级别。其中一个结构在夹紧状态下最大刚度提升可达140%。圆形链甲结构刚度分布最均匀，密集布局可获得更高峰值刚度，同时该界面对底层几何结构有良好的服帖性。

Conclusion: 结构织物真空夹持是一种有效且实用的技术，可用于开发形状自适应、刚度可调的触觉显示器，为仿真体检和触诊训练提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Realistic palpation training requires reliable rendering of soft tissue stiffness changes in real time, which is difficult to achieve with conventional simulators. This paper presents a compact, variable-stiffness tactile interface (VSTaI) based on vacuum-induced jamming of 3D-printed structured fabrics. A vacuum-sealed fabric layer is sandwiched between two silicone layers, and stiffness is tuned by regulating internal pressure. Four fabric patterns with different geometric parameters were fabricated and evaluated using force-indentation tests under atmospheric and vacuum conditions. Across the tested pattern and geometry combinations, vacuum jamming increased stiffness significantly, producing an effective modulus from sub-megapascal to megapascal levels. Specifically, one configuration exhibited a stiffness increase of up to 140% under the jammed state. Circular chainmail patterns provided the most spatially uniform distribution of tactile stiffness, while denser geometries reached higher peak stiffness. VSTaI was also shown to exhibit excellent conformability to the underlying geometry. These results support structured-fabric jamming as a practical approach for shape-conformable, tunable-stiffness displays aimed at physical examination training.

</details>


### [135] [ActFovea: Runtime Safeguarding for VLA Policies via Spatiotemporal Visual-Action Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29169)
*Wenda Yu,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Xin Li,Jingjing Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ActFovea框架，能够在不更改原有VLA策略的情况下，监测并减弱视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在执行过程中因视觉、状态和动作失配导致的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLA策略在机器人操作任务中表现优异，但在实际运行中很容易受到视觉观测、机器人状态和动作之间时序错配的影响，导致性能下降，亟需一种无需重训练即可增强鲁棒性的校验和保护机制。

Method: ActFovea是一个即插即用型保护框架，利用机器人运动学、内感状态和最近执行的动作生成与动作相关的视觉关注区域，保留与接触相关的区域，识别关键运动路径，屏蔽任务无关的视觉信息。通过评估视觉运动与观测实时性是否与几何、内感及行动转移保持一致，从而检测运行风险；当观测可恢复时，生成候选观测并验证动作序列安全性，若无法可靠恢复，则激活限界安全失败机制。

Result: 在多个LIBERO流程中的封闭环实验表明，ActFovea能使在局部视觉遮挡下的成功率从49.3%提升到90.3%，填补了93.7%的性能缺口，并对动作漂移和视觉延迟分别提升了7.0和9.8个百分点，无影响纯净任务表现。在冻结观测重放下，所有实验均能及时触发安全失败，未出现无保护的失败。

Conclusion: 实验证明，空间-时间视觉与动作一致性可为VLA策略提供有效的运行时保护基础。ActFovea无需修改原有策略即可显著增强VLA策略在各种失配干扰下的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation but remain vulnerable to runtime disturbances that break the temporal alignment among visual observations, robot states, and executed actions. We introduce ActFovea, a plug-and-play safeguarding framework that detects and mitigates such failures without retraining or modifying the underlying VLA policy. ActFovea uses robot kinematics, proprioceptive states, and recent actions to construct action-conditioned foveated regions that retain contact-relevant areas and predicted motion corridors while suppressing task-irrelevant visual content. It detects runtime risks by evaluating whether visual motion and observation freshness remain consistent with geometric, proprioceptive, and action transitions. For recoverable disturbances, ActFovea constructs disturbance-specific candidate observations and accepts a recovery only after verifying the resulting action chunk. When stale or replayed observations make reliable recovery impossible, it invokes a bounded safe-failure procedure. In closed-loop evaluations of $π_0$ across multiple LIBERO suites, ActFovea increases success under localized visual overlays from 49.3\% to 90.3\%, closing 93.7\% of the gap to clean performance. It further improves success under action drift and visual delay by 7.0 and 9.8 percentage points, respectively, while preserving clean-task performance. Under frozen-observation replay, ActFovea triggers timely safe failure in all trials, with no unprotected failures. These results demonstrate that spatiotemporal visual-action consistency provides an effective basis for runtime safeguarding of VLA policies.

</details>


### [136] [CLIFT: Turning Gemini Robotics On-Device into Humanoid Specialists via Non-Invasive Closed-Loop Iterative Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29172)
*Yuxin Chen,Hari Srikanth,Nathan Jew,Menglin Wu,Pengcheng Wang,Junli Ren,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Peng Xu,Jinyu Xie,Thomas Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文研究了在仅开放API接口、不可访问模型内部的前提下，如何对机器人基础模型进行下游任务适配与优化。作者提出了一种名为CLIFT的新方法，通过将部署时的奖励反馈转化为API可用的监督数据，实现了在闭源模型下的闭环迭代优化。实验结果显示该方法显著提升了人形机器人在复杂操控任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然闭源的机器人基础模型具备强大能力，但由于数据专有与不开源，用户难以对其进行针对性适配和深度优化。现有API仅支持监督微调，无法结合强化学习等需要训练信号的闭环方法，尤其在高动态、接触丰富的人形操控任务中更为突显。为解决模型适配与部署表现之间的差距，作者希望探索如何实现在只开放SFT API情况下的高效闭环调优。

Method: 作者在现实人形机器人（Gemini Robotics On-Device, GROD）上开展首批相关实证研究，验证通过管理式API进行监督微调的效果。为提升模型部署水平，提出了“闭环迭代微调（CLIFT）”方法，将实际部署获得的奖励反馈转化为API可用的新监督数据，从而绕过对模型权重、梯度等内部信息的需求，实现闭环优化。

Result: 实验表明，直接通过API SFT微调后的策略优于当前一流的开源VLA（在相同数据条件下），但在复杂操控任务上仍未达到部署级别的掌控能力。通过引入CLIFT，经过两轮“飞轮”闭环迭代，GROD几乎达到了完美任务成功率，显著缩小了之前的表现差距。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了即便在仅开放API、不可见模型内部参数的严苛条件下，通过创新的方法（CLIFT），仍可实现强大的闭环策略优化，为闭源大模型在机器人领域的适配与落地提供了新思路和实践路径。

Abstract: While robot foundation models are growing increasingly capable, the strongest models are typically trained on proprietary data and remain closed-source, limiting downstream users' ability to adapt them to new tasks, embodiments, and deployment settings. Following the LLM community, an emerging access paradigm for closed-weight robot foundation models is the managed supervised fine-tuning (SFT) API, where users submit training data and receive a tuned policy without access to model weights, gradients, or training internals. While such APIs let downstream users leverage powerful proprietary foundation models, they restrict policy improvement to pure imitation, ruling out reinforcement learning and other closed-loop methods that rely on internal training signals. This limitation is particularly acute for agile, contact-rich humanoid manipulation, where the gap between policy outputs and deployed behavior is large due to novel states, action tracking dynamics, latency, and controller-specific failure modes. We study how effective this managed-API regime is for humanoid adaptation, and how closed-loop improvement can be realized within it to push policies toward task mastery. We conduct one of the first empirical studies of managed-API adaptation on a real humanoid, instantiated on Gemini Robotics On-Device (GROD). We find that direct SFT through the API substantially outperforms a leading open-weight VLA trained on the same demonstrations, yet still falls short of deployment-level mastery on agile, contact-rich tasks. To close this gap, we introduce CLIFT: Closed-Loop Iterative Fine-Tuning, which turns deployment-time reward feedback into API-compatible supervised data and enables closed-loop policy improvement without accessing weights, gradients, likelihoods, or losses-pushing GROD to near-perfect success after two flywheel cycles, all without "opening the model box."

</details>


### [137] [MROPE: A Multi-Robot Safe Cooperative Strategy via combined Predictive Safety Filters and Ellipse-based Constraint Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29203)
*Alice Rosetti,Lorenzo Pichierri,Domenico Cappello,Fabrizio Schiano,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MROPE的分层策略，能在复杂环境中高效、安全地部署无人机集群追踪动态目标，通过多个创新算法提升实时性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境下无人机集群追踪动态目标时，既要高效协作，又要保证避障与安全，现有集中式方法存在计算瓶颈与可扩展性差的问题。

Method: 提出MROPE分层架构，将整体协同监控任务与局部严格安全需求解耦，方法包含：1）分布式聚合优化实现集群高层协调；2）分布式一致性算法计算安全区域，将复杂障碍物动态聚合为单一安全椭圆；3）局部预测安全滤波（PSF）用于实时避障。

Result: 通过虚拟仿真与现实实验，验证了框架的可行性，与集中式方法相比，MROPE在实时性和系统可扩展性方面更具优势。

Conclusion: MROPE为无人机集群在复杂环境下的动态目标跟踪提供了高效、可扩展、实时的解决方案，优于传统集中式方法。

Abstract: Deploying drone swarms to track a dynamic target in cluttered environments presents severe computational and safety challenges. We propose MROPE, a hierarchical strategy that decouples the cooperative monitoring mission from strict local safety requirements. To overcome the computational bottlenecks typical of dense spaces, our approach dynamically aggregates complex obstacle geometries into a single safe bounding ellipse for each drone. Methodologically, this architecture is realized by combining distributed aggregative optimization for high-level swarm coordination, a decentralized consensus scheme for the safe area computation, and local Predictive Safety Filters (PSF) for real-time collision avoidance. Virtual and real-world experiments validate the framework, demonstrating superior real-time efficiency and scalability compared to centralized approaches.

</details>


### [138] [Event-Based Upper-Body Humanoid Teleoperation Under Challenging Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29227)
*Haoyu Fu,Zhou Ge,Chengze Li,Chenzhao Sun,Ze Cui,Wenjing Zhou,Xulei Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于神经形态事件相机的实时人体上半身动作模仿框架，解决了RGB相机在高动态范围和快速运动场景下感知能力不足的问题，实现了更低的延迟和更强的鲁棒性，尤其在低光和逆光环境下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的帧式RGB摄像头在高动态范围场景和快速动作下，由于曝光固定，易出现感知瓶颈，如细节丢失和模糊，限制了机器人实时模仿人类动作的效果。

Method: 采用Prophesee EVK4事件相机采集高动态范围、高时间分辨率的视觉事件信号，结合优化的事件累积策略与重力对齐的惯性融合构建感知模块，并利用基于因果性的TWIST运动模块实现在线运动转化，将捕捉到的人体动作实时映射到仿人机器人。

Result: 在嵌入式平台和18自由度仿人上半身机器人上测试，系统端到端延迟为23-34毫秒。在强背光和低照度条件下，比传统RGB方案表现出更优的稳定性和准确性。

Conclusion: 事件摄像头在快速动作或光照极差环境下用于远程操控优势明显，而静态光照充足场景下RGB或多模态传感仍有用武之地。

Abstract: We present a real-time upper-body human-to-humanoid motion imitation framework driven by neuromorphic event-based vision. This work addresses practical perceptual bottlenecks of conventional frame-based RGB sensors, specifically their difficulty in high dynamic range (HDR) scenes and rapid motions due to fixed integration times. By leveraging the Prophesee EVK4 event camera, which operates asynchronously with high temporal resolution and a dynamic range exceeding 120 dB, our system supports stable tracking in conditions where standard vision pipelines degrade, such as severe backlighting and very low light environments below 5 lux. The architecture integrates a low-latency Perception Module, utilizing optimized event accumulation and gravity-aligned inertial fusion, with a causal Motion Module (TWIST) that performs online kinematic retargeting. We validate the system on an embedded NVIDIA Booster T1 platform and an 18-DoF humanoid upper-body setup, demonstrating an end-to-end photon-to-action latency of 23-34 ms and advantages over RGB baselines under our experimental setup. The results indicate a practical trade-off: events can be preferable for fast or poorly lit upper-body teleoperation, whereas well-lit static scenes may favor RGB or hybrid sensing.

</details>


### [139] [TacPrint: A Wearable Fingertip Tactile Sensor for Human-to-Robot Contact Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29231)
*Yongxi Liu,Chaofan Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Xiangyin Bao,Boyue Zhang,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可穿戴的指尖触觉传感器TacPrint，实现了高分辨率的触觉信息采集，显著提升了机器人在复杂接触任务中人机技能迁移的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人技能获取过程中，缺乏低成本、可扩展并且不影响自然操作的高精度触觉传感系统，制约了复杂接触类任务中人到机器技能迁移的可靠性。

Method: 作者设计了一款可穿戴指尖传感器TacPrint，将硅胶皮肤内表面突起与24个电容传感单元一一对应，实现了局部化电容响应。利用real-to-sim-to-real管道，将24通道电容信号转化为35×26的接触深度图，采用仿真标注训练和真实信号验证模型表现。

Result: 模型在仿真标注下，实现了优秀的接触区域RMSE（0.223±0.161 mm）、加权质心误差（1.213±2.379像素）以及IoU（0.829±0.169）。实际测量中，深度预测平均绝对误差为0.085±0.057 mm，位置误差为0.250±0.208 mm。基于触觉反馈的人-机重放任务中，抓取和擦拭成功率由0%提升到91.67%和90%；闭环抓取任务，密集深度反馈成功率为87.5%（边缘接触条件下为85%），明显高于原始电容信号反馈。

Conclusion: TacPrint实现了高分辨触觉感知，提高了基于触觉的人机技能迁移的效率和可靠性，将推动机器人在复杂接触任务中的实用性和智能化水平。

Abstract: Human-centric data collection is emerging as a significant paradigm for robot skill acquisition, but seamlessly integrating low-cost, scalable tactile sensing systems that capture fine-grained fingertip interactions without compromising natural operation remains a key challenge. This reduces the reliability of human-to-robot transfer in contact-rich tasks. In this work, we present TacPrint, a wearable fingertip tactile sensor, where protrusions on the inner surface of the silicone skin are aligned one-to-one with 24 capacitive taxels to enable localized capacitive responses. A real-to-sim-to-real pipeline estimates a 35 $\times$ 26 contact-depth map from 24-channel capacitive signals. Against simulation-generated labels, the model achieved a contact-region RMSE of 0.223 $\pm$ 0.161 mm, a weighted-centroid error of 1.213 $\pm$ 2.379 pixels, and an IoU of 0.829 $\pm$ 0.169. With measured capacitive inputs, the network-predicted depth evaluated at the guide-calibrated contact center showed a mean absolute error of 0.085 $\pm$ 0.057 mm across all 40 controlled trials, while the mean contact-position error was 0.250 $\pm$ 0.208 mm across the 37 trials whose reference contact regions were not truncated by the sensing boundary. In human-to-robot replay, tactile-guided compensation increased grasping and wiping success rates from 0% to 91.67% and 90%, respectively. In closed-loop grasping, dense-depth feedback achieved success rates of 87.5% over all tested positions and 85% under edge-contact conditions, compared with 67.5% and 45% for raw-taxel feedback.

</details>


### [140] [FBFM: A Training-Free Asynchronous Feedback Mechanism for Flow-Matching in World-Action Models Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29235)
*Peize Li,Ruimeng Zhang,Ru Zhang,Cong Huang,Kai Chen,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了FBFM机制，通过更细粒度的反馈纠正世界-动作模型（WAMs）长时预测误差，无需重新训练，显著提升机器人视觉规划的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs在长时机器人控制中，为抑制误差累积，依赖于基于真实观测的数据分块更新（chunk-wise feedback）。但这种粗粒度方式不能及时地细致修正每一步的预测误差，影响准确性和事件响应。

Method: 提出一种训练时无需求变动的推理机制——Feedback Flow Matching（FBFM）。该方法在模型生成一段动作后，利用执行结果后的真实观测来矫正下一个动作块的生成，并将跨块反馈引入到当前chunk，形成异步纠错回路。采用masked pseudoinverse方式对条件速度场进行修正。机制支持多种WAM（如DreamZero和LingBot-VA）。

Result: FBFM在LIBERO和RoboTwin2.0等任务上使得成功率提升超过5%。机器人实物观测-预测对比显示，在长期任务中，FBFM能有效抑制漂移，追踪表现明显优于基线方法。

Conclusion: FBFM实现了细粒度的在线反馈纠错，无需对模型重新训练。它为连接开环生成与闭环实际动态提供了新范式，显著提升了机器人长时任务的可靠性和适应性。

Abstract: Although world-action models (WAMs) enhance long-horizon robot control by predicting visual evolution before acting, long-horizon reliability demands repeated re-grounding in real observations--not recursive rollout. Existing WAMs address this by refreshing history or KV cache with ground-truth data between chunks. However, such chunk-wise feedback operates at a coarse temporal granularity and thus fails to correct prediction errors at the individual time-step level. To address this, we propose Feedback Flow Matching (FBFM), a training-free inference mechanism that pushes re-grounding inside the actively generated chunk. During flow matching, FBFM applies a masked pseudoinverse correction to the conditional velocity field: it leverages the preceding action chunk to guide generation of the next action chunk, and uses the image observed after executing that preceding chunk to guide the next frame prediction. This cross-chunk pairing--where feedback from one chunk arrives in time to shape the next--creates an asynchronous loop that corrects errors without waiting for chunk boundaries. Being training-free, the mechanism improves responsiveness to unexpected events and suppresses drift in long-horizon tasks. We evaluate FBFM on both a joint-generation WAM (DreamZero) and a stage-wise WAM (LingBot-VA). On selected LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0 tasks, it improves success rates by over 5% in favorable settings, and real-world robot observation-prediction diagnostics show notably better tracking. We argue that FBFM offers a new paradigm for fine-grained online correction, bridging open-loop flow generation with closed-loop real-world dynamics.

</details>


### [141] [MDIR: A Task-Manifold Impedance Retargeting Method for Contact-Rich Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29271)
*Liu Jiahao,Kento Kawaharazuka,Tasuku Makabe,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种能自动调整阻抗参数的控制方法MDIR，相比于传统固定阻抗，能在机器人任务中减少冲击和力的波动，同时任务完成率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定笛卡尔阻抗控制同时保证了进展和接触支持，但其增益参数难以兼顾安全和力控稳定，实际操作中易导致受力大和不稳定，阻碍复杂任务的演示和复现。

Method: 作者提出了MDIR（流形分解阻抗重定向）方法，仅需单次示范，通过将原始的笛卡尔阻抗指令序列转化为流形上的可执行变阻抗命令。步骤包括：先在操作工作、用力、支撑等通道分解原始响应，并利用被动残差补偿；其次计算一个基线（C2M）映射；最后通过流形约束参数优化（MPO）得到可行解，从而在力峰值、脉冲、力波动和功耗上优化表现。

Result: 在Franka Panda机器人上，对平面擦拭、搬运、推挤等任务共15次闭环实验，MDIR控制通过全部任务检测，并在四项“攻击性”指标（力峰值、冲击、力波动和控制器功耗）上，相比固定阻抗显著降低。

Conclusion: MDIR显著提升了示范性机器人任务的可复现性和安全性，能在不牺牲任务完成度的前提下减少机械臂执行过程中的危险力和能耗，适合需要精细力量控制和高任务安全的场景。

Abstract: Fixed Cartesian impedance makes contact-rich teleoperation demonstrations practical, but gains that secure progress and contact support also determine impact and force variability. We study single-demonstration controller-to-controller impedance retargeting. Given one fixed Cartesian impedance command sequence {K0, D0, xcmd}, Manifold-Decomposed Impedance Retargeting (MDIR) deterministically reparameterizes the recorded controller into an executable task-channel variable-impedance command. MDIR targets this local retargeting problem by preserving projected task-channel responses near the demonstrated trajectory. It represents the source response in operational work, exertion, and support channels with a passive residual complement under a control-chain metric, computes an executable Cartesian-to-Manifold Retargeting (C2M) baseline, and applies Manifold-Constrained Parameter Optimization (MPO) to select a feasible representative with lower wrist-force peaks, impulse, force variability, and nominal controller power. Across planar wiping, pick-and-place, and pushing on a Franka Panda, the full MDIR controller passes Task Check in all 15 closed-loop executions and reduces all four aggressiveness metrics relative to the fixed-impedance demonstrations.

</details>


### [142] [TRACT: Temporally Routed Action Chunks with Chronological Phase Authority for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29285)
*Jiahao Liu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Tasuku Makabe,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRACT方法，通过分解带阶段结构的action chunking，有效减少了机器人模仿学习中决策时延，提升了任务表现，并减少了时序歧义和滞后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人模仿学习在进行action chunking时，若一个chunk跨越了阶段边界，简单地赋予整个chunk当前阶段标签，会造成时序结构不匹配和执行歧义。需要更准确地匹配时序结构和提升动作连贯性。

Method: TRACT方法将阶段结构action chunking分解为当前阶段和未来区间内仅有一次CURRENT-to-NEXT边界转移。构建了任务局部图，用以约束阶段的时间权威，并通过累积边界分布对未来查询进行阶段特异性路由。在接触执行时，引入因果响应亏损积分器以比较策略意图和实际响应，补偿抑制方向的动作，确认补偿后递减修正。

Result: 在6种真实机器人、每种10次实验中，完整TRACT方法获得10/10成功率，擦拭完成率为99.00%（中位数），无阶段歧义与无滞后。与无路由结构相比，成功率（6/10 vs 3/10）和完成率（77.08% vs 8.03%）均优，阶段歧义（8/10降至0/10）和滞后（4/10降至0/10）显著减少。

Conclusion: TRACT方法通过阶段结构和任务局部路由，有效提升了机器人模仿学习中复杂任务的表现，增强了时序协调性，减少了歧义和动作卡顿。而这种表现提升来源于整体方法包，未完全剥离路由机制的贡献。

Abstract: Action chunking shortens the effective decision horizon of robot imitation learning by predicting multiple future actions, while conventional phase conditioning describes the current control instant. When a predicted horizon crosses a procedural boundary, assigning the current phase to the entire chunk creates a structural temporal mismatch. We present TRACT, which factorizes phase-structured action chunking into an accepted current phase and a single CURRENT-to-NEXT boundary inside the future horizon.
